## Moody Painterly Portrait

> Sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A dramatic, medium-shot portrait of a {argument name="subject" default="woman with dark hair styled in a messy updo"}, loose strands framing her face. She is captured in profile, looking back over her shoulder with a thoughtful expression. She wears a {argument name="clothing" default="textured mustard-yellow button-down shirt"}, and the {argument name="lighting" default="warm side-lighting"} highlights her features against a dark, moody textured background, creating a cinematic and painterly atmosphere.
```

## Hyper-Realistic Facial Identity Preservation Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use my uploaded image as the exact facial reference. Preserve my identity with maximum accuracy, including my face shape, hairstyle, beard, eyebrows, skin tone, eye proportions, nose, lips, jawline, ears, and every unique facial feature. Do not alter my age, expression, or recognizable appearance. Create an ultra-realistic, cinematic side-profile portrait with my head gently tilted upward, eyes peacefully closed, conveying calmness, confidence, and deep introspection. Dress me in a premium black textured knit sweater with a clean, minimal aesthetic. Place me against a deep matte-black studio background filled with soft drifting smoke and subtle atmospheric haze. Use dramatic low-key lighting with a gentle rim light outlining my hair, beard, and jawline, while a soft key light elegantly illuminates my face, creating rich contrast and depth. Keep the composition minimal and luxurious, emphasizing negative space. Capture ultra-sharp skin texture, realistic beard details, natural hair strands, and flawless facial proportions. Professional editorial photography, 85mm lens, f/1.8, shallow depth of field, HDR, ultra-photorealistic, premium color grading, soft cinematic shadows, IMAX-quality, 8K resolution, masterpiece, award-winning portrait, no text, no watermark, no artifacts, no facial distortion, no extra limbs, no blur.
```

## Woman with Fluffy Black Cat Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use face image of the uploaded photo with the exact facial proportions and facial structure. Create an ultra-high-definition, photorealistic vertical portrait photograph of a young woman posing closely beside a large, fluffy black long-haired cat, recreating the same composition, body position, body angle, facial orientation, framing, and intimate selfie-style perspective as the reference image. Subject & Pose: The woman is positioned on the right side of the frame, with her upper body angled slightly toward the camera while her head is subtly turned and tilted toward the viewer. Her shoulders are relaxed, with her right shoulder slightly closer to the camera. Her face is captured in a three-quarter frontal angle, looking directly into the camera with a calm, neutral, slightly dreamy expression. Her chin is gently lowered. Her head and upper torso occupy the upper-right and center-right portion of the frame. She is standing or sitting very close beside the cat, creating a natural companion portrait. The cat is positioned prominently on the left side of the frame, sitting upright and facing directly toward the camera. Face & Skin: Render a realistic human face with extremely detailed natural skin texture, subtle visible pores, fine peach fuzz, delicate skin micro-texture, realistic skin translucency, soft tonal variations, natural under-eye texture, faint facial imperfections, and lifelike highlights. Avoid plastic-looking or overly airbrushed skin. The complexion is fair with a soft warm-neutral undertone and naturally luminous appearance. Preserve realistic facial proportions and subtle asymmetry. Eyes & Makeup: Large light hazel-green eyes with detailed irises, natural catchlights, realistic reflections, and fine eyelashes. Soft brown eyeliner is subtly applied along the upper lash line, with delicate mascara definition. The eyebrows are naturally shaped and softly filled with warm medium-brown tones. Makeup is minimal and refined: soft peachy-beige eyeshadow, subtle blush across the cheeks and nose, and naturally glossy muted rose-pink lips with a soft satin finish. The makeup should look like realistic contemporary editorial beauty makeup rather than heavy cosmetics. Hair: The woman has dark brown hair, rich auburn tones with subtle warm highlights. Her hairstyle is an intentionally loose, slightly messy updo, with the majority of her hair gathered into a low, relaxed bun positioned toward the back-right side of her head. The hair has natural volume and realistic individual strands. She has long, wispy, slightly separated curtain-style bangs falling naturally across her forehead and framing both sides of her face. A few loose strands extend around her temples, cheeks, ears, and neck. Reproduce highly detailed individual hair fibers, natural flyaways, soft shine, and realistic strand separation. Outfit: She wears an oversized off-white/cream white graphic T-shirt with a relaxed, slightly slouchy fit. The neckline is wide and loosely stretched, falling off one shoulder and exposing part of the shoulder and collarbone. Underneath, a thin black spaghetti-strap top is visible. The T-shirt features bold black graphic typography and large star illustrations across the front. The visible typography should resemble the reference composition, with large black lettering and graphic elements, while keeping the design naturally integrated into the fabric. The shirt has realistic cotton texture, subtle wrinkles, folds, and natural draping around the torso and shoulders. Accessories: She wears several layered silver-tone chain necklaces of different thicknesses and link styles. One necklace features a prominent silver heart-shaped pendant, while another longer chain has a second dark silver or metallic heart pendant. Include a thin purple cord or necklace mixed among the layered chains. The jewelry should have realistic metallic reflections, small scratches, and polished silver highlights. Cat: Beside her is a large, majestic long-haired black cat with a thick, luxurious coat. The cat is sitting upright on the left side of the composition, facing directly toward the camera. Its fur is predominantly deep black with subtle charcoal-brown highlights visible under the light. The chest and neck have a particularly thick, fluffy mane with individual strands clearly defined. The cat has striking yellow-green/golden eyes with realistic pupils and detailed reflective catchlights. Its ears are upright and alert, and its expression is calm and composed. Render extremely detailed individual fur strands, especially around the face, cheeks, ears, chest, and whiskers. Background & Setting: The scene takes place indoors beside a large floor-to-ceiling window. On the far left, the window allows soft natural daylight into the room, with a bright, slightly overexposed exterior view and a subtle blurred cityscape visible in the distance. The window has dark vertical framing. Behind the woman on the right is a neutral-toned interior with soft beige or taupe curtains featuring subtle vertical folds. The overall environment feels like a modern apartment or bedroom with a cozy, intimate atmosphere. Keep the background naturally blurred while retaining enough detail to establish the setting. Lighting: Use soft natural daylight coming primarily from the large window on the left, creating gentle illumination across the woman's face and the cat's fur. Add subtle directional highlights along the hair, cheekbones, nose, and jewelry. Maintain realistic shadow gradients and soft contrast. The light should feel like a naturally captured indoor photograph rather than studio lighting. Camera & Composition: Shot as a vertical 3:4 close-up environmental portrait / intimate selfie-style photograph, approximately 24–28mm equivalent wide-angle smartphone lens, similar to a modern flagship smartphone camera such as an iPhone 17 Pro Max. Camera positioned approximately at eye level, very close to the subjects, with a slight natural handheld perspective. The woman occupies the right half of the frame while the cat occupies the left half, both faces positioned prominently near the upper-middle portion of the image. The framing includes the woman's head, shoulders, upper torso, and part of her shirt, while capturing most of the cat's upper body and fluffy chest. Use natural smartphone computational photography, realistic HDR, balanced exposure, subtle depth separation, accurate skin tones, crisp facial detail, and realistic lens rendering. Maintain a shallow-to-moderate depth of field, keeping both the woman's face and cat's face sharply focused while the distant window and curtains gradually soften into natural background blur. Image Quality & Realism: Ultra-photorealistic, 8K-quality detail, highly accurate anatomy, authentic human skin, visible pores and fine facial texture, realistic hair fibers, individual cat fur strands, natural fabric texture, physically accurate metallic jewelry reflections, realistic glass and window reflections, subtle photographic grain, high dynamic range, natural color science, professional editorial photography, lifelike details, authentic photographic imperfections, no artificial plastic skin. Negative Prompt: cartoon, anime, illustration, painting, CGI, 3D render, digital art, doll-like face, plastic skin, waxy skin, excessive airbrushing, oversmoothing, beauty filter, unrealistic eyes, deformed facial features, asymmetrical eyes, distorted hands, extra fingers, malformed anatomy, duplicated cat, extra limbs, blurry face, low-resolution, oversaturated colors, excessive makeup, exaggerated body proportions, unnatural hair, fake-looking fur, text distortion, warped jewelry, artificial lighting, extreme bokeh, fisheye distortion, overprocessed HDR, uncanny valley, unrealistic skin texture. Aspect Ratio: 3:4 — ultra-photorealistic close-up editorial smartphone portrait.
```

## Cinematic Night Meadow Flash Portrait

> Sinematik, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Do not change the facial features, proportions, hair color, or skin tone. Maintain 100% likeness. Preserve natural knitted fabric texture, correct human anatomy, exactly two arms, exactly two hands, exactly two legs, and exactly two feet. Each hand must have exactly five fingers with no duplicated limbs, extra fingers, extra legs, AI artifacts, or plastic-looking skin. Keep natural human imperfections.

The uploaded reference image controls everything except the woman's identity. The camera angle must remain almost perfectly side-on (85–90°), and the composition should match the reference almost exactly. The woman should remain relatively small within the frame, while the meadow dominates the composition. The scene should show a {argument name="environment" default="night meadow"} with a {argument name="sky condition" default="dark sky"} and soft moonlight touching the grass. The blanket should remain barely visible. The overall atmosphere should feel calm, nostalgic, and cinematic, capturing the silence of the night and cool air. The colours should remain muted, slightly dark, and film-like, with a deep nighttime palette and subdued tones.

The time of day is deep night, with a dark sky, faint moonlight barely reaching the meadow, and long shadows created by the grass. A camera flash hits directly on the girl, creating sharp directional lighting that pulls her figure out of the darkness, with deep shadows behind her and the background fading into the night. Her skin should reflect the flash with a glassy oil-skin effect, featuring natural highlights on the cheekbones, nose, forehead, and shoulders while preserving realistic skin pores and texture. The girl suddenly turns her head toward the camera, creating motion blur from the movement, with slight blur on the hair and edges of the face while keeping her identity recognizable and her gaze captured mid-motion.

Use high ISO settings with realistic digital sensor noise, slight flash overexposure on the figure and nearby grass, and a realistic RAW photography feel. The final image should be almost indistinguishable from a genuine Canon EOS R5 RAW lifestyle photograph captured in a quiet park at night. Ultra photorealistic, cinematic realism. The manicure should be {argument name="nails" default="short square-shaped milky French style"}.

--ar 9:16 --style raw --style motion 50
```

## Traditional Masterpiece Sketch Technique

> Elle çizilmiş portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Masterpiece, best quality, 8k, ultra sharp, PURE ART STYLE ONLY, NO SUBJECT, NO POSE, NO CHARACTER:

Traditional hand-drawn portrait sketch technique on {argument name="paper type" default="aged warm off-white / cream beige kraft paper"} with subtle visible paper fiber grain texture across the entire surface. Strict monochrome palette only: pure jet black, full rich range of soft gray tones, and the natural cream paper color used as the brightest highlight value — no pure white, no other colors anywhere.

LINE TECHNIQUE (EXACT MATCH):
- Mix of three mediums in perfect balance: smooth graphite pencil, soft smudged charcoal, and fluid black ink
- Hair rendered with extremely dynamic, long, flowing, fast directional brush and pencil strokes, many loose stray wispy lines extending freely outside the main form, highly varied line weight from razor-thin delicate strands to bold thick dark masses, intentionally imperfect and spontaneous
- Facial features drawn with refined, soft, subtle contour lines that disappear and reappear naturally, never a harsh continuous outline, gentle pressure variation creating lifelike sensitivity
- Clothing and lower areas use bold, rough, angular, broken brush strokes with visible dry-brush texture, many areas intentionally left incomplete and dissolving into the paper
- Visible sketch construction lines subtly showing through in lighter areas as part of the aesthetic

SHADING & BRUSH WASH TECHNIQUE (EXACT MATCH):
- Large shadow areas (hair mass, neck, clothing) built from broad, translucent, layered black ink wash / watered-down charcoal washes with soft bleeding edges
- Overlaid on washes: expressive directional hatching and cross-hatching strokes following 3D form curvature
- Facial skin rendered with ultra-smooth blended charcoal gradients, extremely subtle hatching, almost invisible soft transitions for realistic volume, no harsh marks on the face
- Contrast is strong but soft: deep rich near-black areas balanced by delicate mid-gray gradients, with the cream paper itself acting as the natural highlight
- Many hard-edged bold brush strokes placed intentionally next to soft smudged areas for dynamic tension

COMPLETION GRADIENT:
- Central facial area = highly refined, smooth, realistic, fully rendered with precise detail
- Radiating outward and downward = gradually becomes looser, faster, rougher, more abstract, more incomplete, with strokes cutting off abruptly and dissolving naturally into the bare paper background, no hard border to the artwork
- Authentic work-in-progress / sketchbook page aesthetic"

OVERALL AESTHETIC:
Loose, expressive, emotional, confident, painterly sketch feel, clearly hand-drawn by a master artist, fashion illustration meets fine art academic sketch, elegant movement, dynamic energy, no digital smoothness, no vector perfection, every stroke carries intention --ar 9:16 --style raw --stylize 300 --v 6.0, {argument name="clothing accent" default="the clothes are given a soft dark green touch"}
```

Eye Makeup. Large, round, expressive doll-like eyes. Upper lashes are extremely long, full, and dramatically curled in a spiky manga/anime style. Lower lashes are individually defined for a dramatic appearance. Wearing {argument name="lenses" default="large light-grey circle lenses"} with a thin black limbal ring. Thin black eyeliner follows the upper lash line, extending into a subtle outward wing. Aegyosal (Under-Eye Puff). Prominent, full aegyosal beneath the eyes for a youthful and adorable appearance. A thin brown shadow beneath the aegyosal adds depth. A shimmering white highlighter in the centre enhances volume and dimension. Blush. Bright pastel pink. Applied in the Igari (drunken blush) style, beginning beneath the eyes, spreading widely across the upper cheeks, and lightly extending over the bridge of the nose. Creates a strong, sweet, naturally flushed look. Lips. Gradient lips with a full, "blurred lips" effect. Soft light pink around the outer edges, blending into a deeper strawberry red at the centre. Soft semi-matte finish with a subtle natural sheen. A well-defined Cupid's bow. Her hair is very long, light golden brown, soft, healthy, glossy, and highly realistic. It falls naturally with gentle volume, featuring delicate, luminous texture and a few soft wispy strands framing her face. The overall image should feel luxurious, aesthetic, feminine, fresh, natural, and modern, like a professionally photographed selfie. Do not change the facial features, face shape, body proportions, or the colour, length, or texture of the hair. Preserve a 100% resemblance to the original person. Do not make the subject look younger or idealised. Do not alter the eye shape, lips, nose, or chin. Preserve the natural facial expression, skin texture, pores, and all unique individual characteristics.

A photorealistic candid selfie, capturing a spontaneous moment in a vertical frame. A {argument name="subject" default="young woman"} is taking a selfie while leaning slightly toward the phone, which is positioned just below face level, creating a natural low-angle perspective. At the exact moment of capture, she suddenly turns her head to the side as if someone has called her name. Her gaze shifts sideways, and realistic motion blur appears across her face and hair from the quick head movement, while her face remains clearly recognisable. Her smile stays genuine and effortless, making the image feel like an authentic split-second snapshot rather than a posed photograph.

Her long, thick, expressive eyelashes remain partially visible through the subtle motion blur. Her long, voluminous, thick hair flows dramatically in the direction of the head turn, creating elegant motion trails with individual strands. Several loose strands cross her face, enhancing the sense of movement.

She is wearing a {argument name="clothing" default="light, airy, loose-fitting blouse made from thin cotton"} with a natural fabric texture in a soft pastel pink shade. It features voluminous long sleeves gathered gently at the cuffs, a neat rounded neckline with a short placket, and delicate pearlescent buttons. The fabric moves softly in the breeze, forming natural folds. No prints, logos, or unnecessary decorative elements.

Behind her is a lush tree completely covered in blooming {argument name="flowers" default="white lilac flowers"}. The white blossoms remain crisp and clearly defined. A rich, cloudless blue sky creates strong contrast with the white flowers and the soft pink blouse. Several branches are positioned close to the camera, adding natural depth to the composition.

Warm spring backlighting shines through her hair and the blossoms, creating a golden rim light around the hair, soft volumetric shadows, and realistic sun flares. The lighting feels natural, lively, and directional, not like studio lighting.

The image should look like a genuine candid photo taken on a regular smartphone while in motion: natural wide-angle perspective, a slight tilt to the frame, subtle lens imperfections, delicate 35mm film grain, muted and slightly desaturated colours, realistic skin texture with visible pores, and authentic fabric detail. No excessive sharpness, no HDR, and no glossy fashion-editorial look.

--ar 9:16 --raw --style motion 50

Manicure: short, natural oval-shaped nails with a semi-transparent milky pink polish and a natural glossy finish.
```

The black T-shirt should blend seamlessly into the shadows so that the lower chest, arms, and body are almost invisible, leaving the illuminated side of the face as the primary focal point.

Background: Pure matte black with absolutely no texture, patterns, objects, or distractions.

Style: High-contrast monochrome, cinematic fine-art portrait with soft shadow transitions, realistic skin texture, natural hair detail, subtle film grain, and an intensely dark, minimalist atmosphere. Ultra-detailed, museum-quality studio photography, 8K realism.

## Candid Night Smartphone Photography

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic candid smartphone photograph, 9:16 vertical.
Use the attached image only as a strong visual inspiration for general facial proportions and vibe,
while ensuring the generated subject remains fully original, non-identifiable, and not a face copy.
Scene
Nighttime on a quiet {argument name="location" default="Tokyo city sidewalk"} beside a building with white ceramic tile walls.
The subject is walking past the camera, partially side-on, captured mid-step in a rushed, accidental moment.
Poses & Gestures (combined naturally)
The subject reacts instinctively to being photographed:
turning her head back mid-walk as if surprised,
raising one hand close to the lens to partially block the camera,
{argument name="expression" default="shy, restrained smile forming as she suppresses laughter"},
body angled in half-profile while continuing to walk forward,
motion carries her slightly out of frame, imperfect framing.
Expressions are subtle and spontaneous — embarrassed, playful, fleeting — never posed.
Camera & Motion
Shot on a modern smartphone, handheld, rushed capture. Strong uncontrolled camera shake. Heavy directional motion blur across the raised hand, hair, and face. Facial features appear smeared and streaked by movement, faintly recognizable but distorted. Partial ghosting around the body. Imperfect framing, subject nearly leaving the frame.
Lighting
Flash-only lighting fired mid-motion. Harsh flash highlights on skin and hand. Deep surrounding darkness. Uneven exposure, blown highlights, hard shadows typical of hurried night phone photography.
Background
White ceramic tile wall stretches into streaked light bands due to motion blur. Street surroundings fade into darkness with minimal readable detail.
Mood
Candid, shy, slightly playful. Feels intrusive, raw, imperfect, and authentic — like a fleeting memory accidentally captured and never meant to be perfect.
Image Quality
Extremely noisy. Heavily blurred. Raw smartphone look. Not cinematic. Not editorial. Not polished.
Negative Prompt
anime, illustration, painting, stylized,
studio lighting, beauty lighting, soft portrait,
sharp focus, clean face, perfect anatomy, smooth skin,
fashion editorial, posed portrait, cinematic grading, film still,
tripod shot, professional photography,
identity match, face copy, real person replication
```

## Mediterranean Summer Lifestyle Portrait

> Sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A medium waist-up framing captures a confident adult male in mid-stride, his chest facing the camera while his head turns 45 degrees to gaze sharply off-camera to the right. He has a warm tan, bronzed complexion and a meticulously groomed, full, dense dark beard. His dark brown to black hair features a crisp high skin fade on the sides and back, blending into one and a half to two inches of length on top that is styled upward and slightly forward with a textured, matte-finish product, displaying natural thickness and slight, non-uniform variations in strand direction alongside a sharp temple line-up. His serious, guarded expression is accentuated by a closed neutral mouth and dark charcoal sunglasses that completely obscure his eyes. He wears a light cornflower blue button-down shirt paired with cream linen trousers. His left hand rests casually downward by his side with fingers naturally curled, adorned with a thin gold chain bracelet on the wrist and a gold ring on his pinky. His right hand is raised to shoulder height, where the index finger gently hooks over the collar of a light cream linen jacket slung over his slightly raised right shoulder, the thumb and remaining fingers pinching the fabric securely. He stands positioned slightly to the right of center in an outdoor Mediterranean setting, balanced by the negative space of a weathered, center-left midground stone archway. This traditional Apulian Trullo entrance is constructed from rough-hewn raw cut limestone and tufa blocks, featuring unfinished dry-stone masonry with deep crevices, irregular chipped edges, aged pitting, and a matte off-white and cream finish with subtle beige and gray undertones, framing a deep black void inside. In the background at the top right, a weathered conical stepped stone roof rises, while a few blurred thriving olive green leaves drop down into the top left edge of the foreground. A sun-drenched, warm Mediterranean afternoon bathes the scene in bright, soft natural sunlight with strong environmental bounce from the front-left, illuminating the subject's right side with a warm golden glow. Soft, gradual shadows fall under his right arm, along the left side of his face and neck, and deep within the stone archway, while preserved specular highlights accent his right cheek, nose, the upper chest of his blue shirt, and the sunlit edges of the textured stone. The split-complementary palette creates a sophisticated mood of relaxed, polished masculinity. Captured straight on at eye level using digital photography with a 50mm or 85mm lens at an aperture of f/4.0 to f/5.6, the shot features a medium depth of field that keeps the subject tack sharp while allowing the background architecture to become slightly softer at the extreme edges. Post-processing includes a gentle contrast curve, targeted sharpening on the hair, beard, and masonry, and color grading that enhances the warm golden hour tones to beautifully contrast the cool blue shirt against the warm stone and skin, all seamlessly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

The color palette features deep shadows, vibrant orange, turquoise, light blue, white, and gray. Technique: The composition features messy, loose sketch lines and rough charcoal outlines visible beneath the paint. The edges are fragmented with abstract blocks of color, giving the work a bold, "unfinished" look. The background is abstract and minimalist, allowing the textured brushstrokes to stand out. Somber, modern, and atmospheric.

An athletic young man stands casually facing the camera, his weight shifted as he leans slightly with level shoulders and legs crossed at the ankles. A silver chain rests on his neck. His right hand is casually tucked into his front right trouser pocket, revealing only a sliver of his hand and a metallic watch on his wrist, while his left hand lightly grips a weathered dark gray wrought iron fence for balance, his fingers draped elegantly over the rounded cast-iron finial with silver rings visible on his index and pinky fingers, complemented by a silver bracelet on his left wrist. He is positioned along an outdoor lakeside promenade characterized by deep spatial depth, standing on a light gray and white mix gravel path. Directly behind him, extending from the mid-left to the right, is a classic, carved stone balustrade made of muted gray stone, exhibiting rough matte textures, dark water stains under the top rail, and patches of lichen and dirt accumulating in its weathered crevices. To the left midground, a thriving bush bursts with bright pink blossoms and lush green leaves growing through a support structure, situated near a weathered vintage black metal streetlamp with a glass lantern enclosure. Expanding into the right background is a vast expanse of pristine, deep blue lake water marked by gentle ripples, where a small dark motorboat glides in the distance. Above the water, a large sloping mountain range dotted with small distant shoreline structures fades gracefully into an atmospheric haze. Bathed in bright, warm golden natural sunlight originating from a single moderately directional source at the front-left, the scene is infused with a cheerful and sophisticated luxurious vacation mood. The lighting casts soft but defined gradual gray shadows onto the gravel path behind and to the right of the subject, creating raked horizontal shadow patterns from the balustrade while also defining the right side of the folds in his clothing. Preserved specular highlights gently kiss the left side of his face, his left shoulder, and the top rail of the stone balustrade, while ambient fill light ensures vibrant, medium contrast across the beautifully complementary cream and steel blue color palette. Captured through the lens of a full-frame digital camera using a 35mm lens at an aperture around f/5.6, the image maintains a straight-on perspective and a medium depth of field, keeping the subject tack sharp with no grain while the majestic mountainous background and water are slightly softened yet clearly legible. The aesthetic blends modern menswear editorial styles with clean travel influencer photography, finalized with post-processing that includes subtle color grading to enhance the blue and cream tones, a slight shadow lift to preserve details in the dark hair and iron fence, and an accentuation of the golden hour warmth radiating from the subject's skin, all beautifully framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.

## Intense Low-Key Studio Portrait

Wearing a {argument name="shirt" default="tight white dress shirt"} with sleeves rolled up to the elbows, and {argument name="trousers" default="dark fitted trousers"}. Forearms must be clean - no tattoos, markings, or skin alterations.

Lighting: dramatic low-key lighting with a deep black background. Key light from camera-right, slightly above eye level, creating strong contrast and sculpted shadows across the face and arms. Subtle negative fill on camera-left to deepen shadows, with minimal soft fill near camera for faint catchlights in the eyes.

Camera settings: shot on an {argument name="lens" default="85mm lens"}, f/2.0, ISO 200, 1/125s. Focus: subject sharp with shallow depth of field, background completely black and non-distracting.

Texture fidelity: preserve natural skin texture and pores (no plastic smoothing), realistic fabric tension on the shirt, subtle highlights on skin, and clean tonal transitions.

Composition: vertical portrait framing, centered subject, tight crop around torso and head, cinematic balance with strong presence.

Color & grading: deep blacks with preserved detail, neutral tones, controlled highlights, no clipping. Subtle film grain, clean editorial retouching, high contrast, powerful and introspective mood, premium studio portrait quan
Ratio 4:5
```

ENVIRONMENT: Modern minimalist bathroom — white tiles, a white ceramic sink, a rounded mirror, a few grooming products on the counter (shaving cream, aftershave, face wash), a small hand towel, a glass of water. SKIN: Hyper-realistic with very visible pores, facial hair texture, micro-imperfections, slight T-zone oiliness, sleep marks on cheek, slight redness around nose, real human texture. A tiny ingrown hair or razor bump on the neck. Fine lines starting around eyes. NO smoothing, NO beauty filter, NO airbrushing. Maximum authentic morning skin realism. CAMERA: iPhone front camera mirror selfie, 9:16 vertical, slight angle. Sharp focus on his face. Authentic phone grain, slight chromatic noise typical of fluorescent light photography. STYLE: Real candid morning bathroom selfie, mundane and authentic. The kind of photo you take to send a friend, not to post on Instagram. Vulnerable, real, completely human. Zero AI artifacts.

The scene is bathed in a sophisticated mix of soft, diffused natural daylight from the front-left and top, blending seamlessly with warm golden ambient practical fill. This moderately directional light creates beautifully preserved highlights along the left side of his face, his left shoulder, and the edge of the balustrade railing, while casting short, softly graded gray shadows beneath him on the parquet floor and across the right side of his suit and face. The color palette is warmly analogous, harmonizing the dominant mocha browns, rich black accents, warm beiges, and the amber glow of the lighting to evoke an affluent, classically elegant, and highly polished mood. Captured as a tack-sharp, grain-free editorial fashion photograph blending modern menswear with classic architectural portraiture, the image is shot straight on using an 85mm lens at an f/1.8 aperture, 1/200s shutter speed, and ISO 200 to achieve a shallow depth of field with creamy bokeh that smoothly blurs the majestic background. Expert post-processing features warm color grading to emphasize earth tones, a slight contrast bump, and targeted sharpening on the subject, all perfectly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

Ultra-photorealistic vertical 9:16 Instagram Story-style candid portrait of a {argument name="subjects" default="stylish young mother and her toddler daughter"} sitting together inside an {argument name="location" default="Istanbul Marmaray train"}.
The image should feel like a real phone-camera capture: natural perspective, slight casual framing, subtle lens distortion, authentic skin texture, soft cinematic realism, and a warm, intimate everyday moment.

Use the uploaded woman as the main identity reference for the mother.
The toddler girl should look like a little daughter version of the uploaded woman, with similar facial harmony and recognizable resemblance, while still looking like a realistic toddler. She has soft round cheeks, straight dark bangs, two braided pigtails, and an adorable pouty / slightly grumpy expression, similar to the mood of the reference baby image. She is looking at the camera with a cute stubborn facial expression.

Child styling

The little girl is sitting on the Marmaray seat and wears a {argument name="dress style" default="pink, wrapped-style floral patterned summer dress"}, with soft pink and yellow flower details. The dress should feel sweet, summery, and childlike.
She wears pink summer shoes / sandals and carries a small pink-and-yellow handbag, playful and stylish, matching the outfit.
Her proportions, hands, legs, and face must look naturally toddler-like and photorealistic.

Mother styling

Next to her sits her mother, elegant, youthful, and beautiful, in a coordinated pink-and-yellow halter-neck summer dress tied around the neck, with a floral print matching the daughter’s outfit.
The mother’s pose should feel relaxed, warm, protective, and natural, sitting close beside her daughter.
She has softly styled hair, natural glam makeup, delicate jewelry, and a gentle smile. She should look effortlessly chic and feminine, like a real lifestyle Instagram Story capture rather than a studio fashion shoot.

Environment

The setting is clearly inside an Istanbul Marmaray train:
clean modern train carriage, silver metallic seats, blue floor, vertical poles, train doors, soft neutral interior colors, and subtle Marmaray visual cues in the background.
The space should feel realistic, clean, bright, and naturally lived-in, without clutter.

Lighting and mood

Use soft natural indoor transit lighting, slightly warm and flattering, with realistic shadows and highlights.
Mood: intimate, sweet, summery, stylish, realistic, everyday luxury, soft feminine warmth, candid mother-daughter moment in Istanbul.
The overall aesthetic should feel like a real Instagram Story photo: casual, close, warm, authentic, and emotionally charming.

Visual style

Photorealistic, high-resolution, realistic skin texture, real fabric texture, natural anatomy, realistic hands, realistic toddler proportions, subtle cinematic softness, natural depth, no artificial AI glow, no over-smoothing, no waxy skin.

Optional overlay

Add a subtle Instagram Story interface at the top with the username “ozgedoner” for a modern socia

Scene Setup:
The subject lies on a clean {argument name="bed surface" default="Bed Surface Type"}, captured from an overhead or slightly angled overhead perspective, with the face clearly centered and the body naturally extended. The bedsheets and pillows create large areas of negative space. The overall composition evokes a sense of authentic, restrained, intimate bedroom straight flash photography.

Character Setup:
Adult female with a distinctly recognizable appearance, featuring naturally refined facial features without an influencer-like face. Hairstyle is 【Hairstyle】, hair color is 【Hair Color】, and makeup style is 【Makeup Style】. The gaze conveys 【Emotional Keywords】, such as a need for protection, slight surprise, mild anticipation, quietness, vulnerability, clarity, or semi-dazed, but without exaggerated performative elements. Retain authentic skin texture without excessive smoothing.

Clothing Setup:
Outfit is {argument name="outfit" default="Clothing Type"}, such as crop tops, off-shoulder styles, strapless tops, tie-front blouses, short tees, tank tops, knit tops, or silk blouses, leaning toward a fashionable editorial feel with everyday wearability. Pair with minimal metallic accessories, bracelets, rings, or earrings, but avoid over-layering.

Composition:
The subject is presented in central or slightly off-center composition, with the face as the primary visual focus and hands and waistline as secondary focal points. Hair strands naturally splay out to create a sense of line and flow. The image should have a clear main subject, clean background, and stable visual reading path.

Lighting and Texture:
Employ strong straight flash from a phone or camera, resulting in distinct highlights, hard shadows, and authentic flash quality. Bedsheets, skin, and clothing should show clear reflective layers, evoking a genuine moment captured by flash in a nighttime bedroom, avoiding soft studio lighting effects.

Color and Atmosphere:
Dominant color palette is 【Dominant Color Tone】, with low saturation, authenticity, restraint, and cleanliness overall. The background features a white bed, subtle gray shadows, and minimal bedding folds, steering clear of complex props or cluttered scenes. The image's vibe draws from bedroom flash editorial, overhead emotional portraits, and a subtle blend of Y2K with soft grunge aesthetics.

Quality Requirements:
High-definition, authentic photographic feel, natural hand structure, realistic hair strands, accurate body proportions, clear facial details, fine skin textures, and a high-fashion sensibility.
```

​Captured in a medium-full shot from a slightly low angle, she leans casually and confidently forward against a {argument name="location" default="kitchen counter"}, her torso projecting slightly toward the camera. She has her hair gracefully swept to one side, looking directly at the camera with expressive, slightly narrowed eyes and a playful, confident, subtle smile. Her skin features a highly realistic, glowing texture with fine pores visible under the light. She is dressed in a plain, {argument name="clothing" default="white, form-fitting cropped tank top"} that reveals her midriff, paired with high-waisted black dolphin sports shorts featuring white piping and a white drawstring tied in a bow at the front.
​The setting is a modern, well-lit domestic kitchen with a playful, alluring, and casual vibe. The background showcases light brown wooden lower cabinets with silver handles, a black countertop with integrated stove knobs, a white square-tile backsplash, and upper wooden cabinets. Subtle props like hanging kitchen utensils (a spatula and ladle) and a metal sink faucet blend into the elegantly blurred background. Bright, soft natural daylight streams through a large window on the left, casting a delicate rim light on her hair and the left side of her body, while creating smooth, natural shadow transitions on her right side.
​Shot with a 35mm lens at f/2.8 to maintain sharp focus on her eyes and clothing texture while yielding a beautiful background bokeh on the tiles and kitchen details. The lighting is perfectly balanced, simulating ISO 200 and a 1/250s shutter speed to prevent overexposure from the window, ensuring impeccable white balance and perfectly retained shadow details. Aspect Ratio 9:16.
```

## Golden Hour Car Driver Portrait

```
A young male sits centered in the driver's seat of a car, facing the camera with level shoulders in a deliberately posed, confident posture. His short, blonde to light-brown brushed-back hair exhibits natural texture and subtle flyaways. He wears an olive green puffy jacket with a silver zipper layered over a black ribbed zip-up sweater, with a pristine black woven seatbelt crossing his chest. His expression features a subtle, confident closed smile and a direct gaze with a relaxed squint, while his arms extend out of frame, implying he is holding the camera. Surrounding him is the deep spatial interior of the vehicle, constructed of smooth glass and slightly textured matte charcoal black leather and plastic trim. To his right in the midground rests a pristine black leather seat headrest with visible stitching. Overhead, a dominant panoramic sunroof enclosed in a black frame displays perfectly clear glass, while the distant background through the rear side window reveals weathered bare trees, suggesting late autumn or winter. The calm yet energetic mood is driven by highly directional, hard 45-degree golden natural window light that illuminates the right side of his face, placing distinct highlights on his right cheekbone, nose bridge, jawline, and jacket collar. This creates high contrast with deep black, harshly defined shadows falling under his jaw, inside the collar, across the unlit left side of his face, and deep into the car's interior. A split-complementary palette dominates, contrasting his warm skin and the olive green jacket against the charcoal blacks and a bright sky blue expanse filled with white cumulus clouds visible through the sunroof. The tack sharp, realistic digital photograph is captured from a slightly low angle using a smartphone equivalent 24mm lens at f/1.8 to f/2.2, a 1/1000s shutter speed, and ISO 100, featuring a deep depth of field with focus firmly locked on his eyes. Post-processing enhances the golden hour lifestyle aesthetic by deepening the interior blacks, slightly boosting the saturation of the sky and jacket, and applying digital noise grain and sharpening to his facial features and hair, beautifully framed in a 3:4 aspect ratio.
```

A candid, hyper-realistic indoor café scene featuring a {argument name="subject" default="young woman sitting at a dark wooden table"}, facing forward behind an open silver laptop (Apple MacBook with glowing logo). She has fair skin, soft facial features, and light brown hair tied loosely in a messy bun with a few strands falling naturally around her face. She is wearing a {argument name="outfit" default="simple oversized dark gray sweatshirt"}, giving a relaxed and casual look.\
\
Her expression is playful and slightly mischievous, sticking her tongue out subtly while looking directly toward the camera. Her posture is slightly leaned forward, elbows resting near the table, suggesting a relaxed working or studying moment.\
\
The setting is a {argument name="setting" default="cozy, dimly lit café corner"} with warm ambient lighting and smooth brown walls in the background, creating a minimalist and intimate atmosphere. The lighting is soft and diffused, casting gentle shadows and enhancing skin tones naturally.\
\
On the table:\
\
* In the foreground, a white ceramic cup filled with coffee sits on a matching saucer, slightly off-center, closest to the camera, with smooth reflections on the surface.\
* To the left, a second laptop (partially visible) is open, showing a colorful interface on the screen.\
* Near the center-left, a small rectangular purple card lies flat on the table.\
* A white cup and saucer are also visible behind the main laptop, suggesting another drink.\
* On the right side, there is a folded dark textured fabric or bag resting near the edge of the table.\
* Additional subtle objects like napkins and small items are scattered naturally, enhancing realism.\
\
Composition:\
\
* Shot from a slightly low, eye-level perspective across the table, creating depth.\
* Foreground (coffee cup) slightly blurred with shallow depth of field.\
* Midground (laptop and subject) in sharp focus.\
* Background softly blurred.\
\
Style & quality:\
\
* ultra photorealistic, candid photography style\
* natural lighting, warm tones\
* shallow depth of field, soft bokeh\
* realistic textures (skin, fabric, wood, ceramic)\
* high dynamic range, 8K detail\
* no over-editing, authentic lifestyle aesthetic
```

## Urban Lifestyle Portrait at The River Thames

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A waist-up portrait of a young adult male standing perfectly centered and facing the camera directly, exuding a confident yet approachable demeanor with a subtle closed smile. His eyes and eyebrows are completely concealed behind thick-rimmed black acetate sunglasses, framed by dark, matte over-ear headphones. He is clad in a heavy black overcoat layered over a light heather-grey hoodie, his shoulders level in a poised stance. His arms are clasped at his chest in a classic, relaxed crossed-arms posture, where his right hand rests over his left forearm with the fingers slightly curled inward and hidden inside the deep fold of the coat sleeve, while his other hand remains tucked securely beneath his right bicep. Behind him, an outdoor urban setting unfolds with deep spatial layers, beginning with thick, cylindrical metallic suspension cables that stretch diagonally from mid-left to mid-right, providing a smooth, reflective structural framing. The midground features the dark blue-grey water of the flowing River Thames, textured with slight surface ripples and reflections. Stretching across the distant background is a sweeping cityscape mixing weathered brick facades, historic domes, and modern glass blocks, dramatically anchored in the top right quadrant by the towering, pristine pyramidal glass structure of The Shard, detailed with a subtle red crane near its tip. The scene captures a crisp, clear day defined by highly directional, hard natural sunlight striking from the front-left at roughly a 45-degree angle from above. This intense illumination creates a high-contrast, energetic atmosphere, casting deep black shadows with harsh, defined edges under the subject's chin, along the right side of his face, deep inside the interior folds of his black coat, and across the right side of his hoodie. Stark highlights are perfectly preserved on his left cheek, the top rim of the grey hoodie, and the gleaming metallic bridge cables. The complementary color profile relies heavily on the striking contrast between the highly saturated clear blue sky—which transitions in a smooth gradient from a darker zenith to a lighter horizon dotted with scattered fluffy white clouds in the mid-left quadrant—and the neutral, deep tones of his urban streetwear. Rendered in the clean, structured, and realistic style of modern lifestyle influencer photography, the digital image boasts tack-sharp focus on the subject while employing a medium depth of field that leaves the distant background architecture slightly soft yet distinctly recognizable. The straight-on, eye-level perspective mimics a standard portrait lens shot at a fast shutter speed, enhanced by high-contrast post-processing that slightly crushes the blacks to emphasize the rich texture of the overcoat while keeping the vivid blue hues in the sky exceptionally vibrant, with the entire composition beautifully framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

## Vintage Hollywood Monochrome Portrait

```
A young adult male sits confidently facing the camera, his weight comfortably shifted with relaxed shoulders, exuding a sophisticated aura with a subtle, closed smile as his relaxed eyes gaze off-camera to the right. He features short, slicked-back glossy hair that catches the light, complemented by slight facial stubble, and is dressed in an oversized, highly textured dark gray double-breasted suit layered effortlessly over a crisp, bright white t-shirt. His left hand rests casually on the armrest, fingers spread naturally and draped downward over the edge, revealing a dark-dial wrist watch, while his right hand rests gently on his own right thigh with the fingers loosely curled inward. He sits upon a vintage, dark mottled crushed velvet sofa in the foreground that anchors the composition, transitioning into a midground that features a heavy vintage throw pillow adorned with thick, metallic-looking fringe and dark patterned fabric directly beneath his left hand. The medium-depth background reveals a pristine, upscale lounge setting highlighted by an elegant matte wallpaper featuring an Art Deco repeating fan and shell motif inside arched geometric lines in a uniform black, white, and mid-gray pattern, meticulously enclosed within dark wood wall molding panel frames. The entire atmosphere is rendered in monochromatic, high-contrast black and white, defined by a single, highly directional, bright artificial hard light source positioned straight from the front. This intense, direct-flash illumination creates a dramatic, moody vintage Hollywood glamour aesthetic, casting harsh, deep black, defined short shadows under his chin, within the deep folds of the suit fabric, and throwing a sharp, distinct cast shadow directly onto the wall behind his head, while simultaneously preserving brilliant specular highlights on his forehead, nose, cheeks, the glossy sheen of his hair, the white t-shirt, and the metallic watch dial. Captured with the tack-sharp realism of direct-flash editorial photography using a 35mm equivalent lens at an f/5.6 or f/8 aperture for a deep depth of field, the straight-on perspective is refined through monochromatic post-processing with pushed contrast, crushed deep blacks, and a subtle 400 ISO film grain texture enhancing the facial features and clothing, all perfectly framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

A male in his mid-30s stands center-left in a confident, medium waist-up pose, his body angled 45 degrees away from the camera to face the right. He features medium-length wavy hair meticulously styled into a pushed-back quiff with a subtle fade, complemented by a sharply groomed beard, while dark tortoiseshell sunglasses with amber-tinted lenses conceal his eyes as he looks off-camera. His posed expression is serious and composed, characterized by a closed mouth and neutral eyebrows. He is elegantly dressed in a heavy, dark espresso brown double-breasted overcoat worn over a true black ribbed turtleneck and black trousers, his shoulders kept perfectly level and structured by the thick wool. With his weight shifted slightly back, his right hand rests casually inside his front right trouser pocket. Anchoring the left foreground is a weathered, warm gray-tan stone parapet exhibiting a raw, matte finish heavily textured with natural pitting, scuffs, and a curved top edge. Beyond the subject, the midground opens up to the smooth, slate blue waters of the Arno River, reflecting the sky before receding into a deep, softly blurred background. Dominating this distant plane is a historic arched stone bridge lined with vintage yellow and ochre residential buildings, their small windows and classical architecture evoking the timeless essence of Florence. The scene is bathed in the golden, highly directional natural sunlight of late afternoon, emanating from a 45-degree front-left angle to strike with hard, bright intensity and slightly diffused edges. This single light source creates a dramatic, high-contrast atmosphere, carving deep black shadows into the intricate fabric folds of his coat, under his chin, and heavily across the right side of his face and torso. Perfectly placed specular highlights gleam across his left cheek, the bridge of his nose, the textured crown of his hair, the curvature of his sunglasses, and his left shoulder. The sophisticated, travel-inspired European mood is unified by a warm split-complementary palette of espresso, true black, amber, and slate blue. Rendered as a realistic digital photograph inspired by Pitti Uomo street style, the image is shot straight-on at eye level with a full-frame camera and an 85mm lens set to f/2.8, utilizing a fast 1/500s shutter speed at ISO 100 to ensure the subject is tack sharp against the smooth bokeh. Precise post-processing deepens the blacks, sharpens the facial and garment textures, and applies a subtle split tone that warms the highlights and cools the shadows, all beautifully framed in a 4:5 aspect ratio.
```

CAMERA / SMARTPHONE LOGIC: Shot on an iPhone 15 Pro front camera using direct flash. Ultra-sharp smartphone rendering with subtle computational artifacts. No DSLR blur. No cinematic grading. Visible smartphone characteristics: direct flash flattening facial shadows and brightening nearby skin, minor edge sharpening around cap embroidery, lashes, hair strands, lips, and fingers, slight front-camera facial widening from close distance, tiny JPEG softness in dark curtain folds, slight highlight clipping on nose bridge, cheek, lips, and fingertips, noise reduction smoothing in shadow-heavy background areas, natural handheld framing with imperfect close crop. CANDID MOVEMENT: Subject should feel naturally paused mid-rest: cheek softly place at the palm, fingers slightly uneven against the face, lips naturally parted, cap sitting low and imperfect, hair strands falling randomly across cheeks and shoulders. Keep face sharp while allowing subtle softness on loose hair tips and curtain background. LIGHTING: Harsh compact phone flash mixed with dim bedroom ambient light. Foreground face and hand appear bright while curtains and bedding fall into dark muted shadows. SKIN / TEXTURE: Natural pale skin with visible pores, soft cheek warmth, realistic under-eye depth, glossy lip texture, and mild flash-induced smoothing on brightest areas. Preserve tactile realism instead of porcelain AI skin. HAIR / MAKEUP: Long dark slightly messy hair with wispy bangs and flyaways. Soft Korean-inspired makeup with glossy pink lips, subtle blush, thin eyeliner, defined lashes, natural brows, and faint aegyo-sal catching flash. OUTFIT / STYLING: {argument name="clothing" default="Black baseball cap with white embroidered logo, simple beige tank top straps"}, minimal jewelry. Styling should feel casual, private, and late-night rather than fashion-editorial. ENVIRONMENT: Dim bedroom with dark curtains, soft bedding, and shadow-heavy background. Space should feel quiet, personal, and lived-in without luxury studio polish. COMPOSITION: Close-up selfie framing with subject centered slightly low. Cap brim creates curved shadow geometry over forehead. Hand and forearm dominate lower center from close camera distance. Hair frames both sides of the face. ## Modern Office Hijab Selfie

> Fotogerçekçi, retro logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high-angle selfie of a {argument name="subject" default="young woman wearing a black hijab"}, posing casually inside a {argument name="setting" default="modern office environment"}. She is wearing round sunglasses with thin frames. Her facial expression shows a playful duck face (pout lips), giving a cute, slightly flirty and soft vibe.
Outfit: a {argument name="outfit" default="black jacket with a vintage logo patch on the front and a brown corduroy collar"}, paired with a finely pleated black skirt. She is holding a green matcha latte in a transparent plastic cup with a straw in her right hand.
Background: a realistic office setting such as a workspace or break area, featuring desks, office chairs, computers/laptops, and small items like files or stationery. The floor may be tiled or carpeted. Through the window, there’s a slightly wet, post-rain atmosphere outside, creating a fresh mood. Modern glass elements or office partitions are visible.
Composition: the image feels spontaneous, like a real phone selfie. The framing is slightly imperfect and natural, giving a candid, unposed look.
Lighting: a mix of soft natural light from the window and indoor white office lighting, balanced and not too harsh.
Focus: sharp focus on the face and outfit, with a shallow depth of field (slightly blurred background).
Style: ultra-realistic, candid selfie, iPhone camera look, natural lighting, high-detail skin texture, aesthetic daily life, slightly grainy, real-life vibe, 4K.
```
